# Warlord (realm) de jure; counties first join the medieval set (gen_medieval.py) in 4677.1.1, or 5476.1.1 (West) / 5641.1.1 (East)
# from the ancient layers, and come to these titles by region from 5936.1.1 (build_base_world.py): every county goes back
# to its old duchy and every old realm title (the warlord titles) to its old liege. Before it the base duchies serve the Bronze (from 1700) and Classical (from 3800) titles; at the 100.1.1 test
# start, the <tier>_base_<name> copies in 02_LR_base_titles.txt hold the land. Iran, India and Gaul: LR_iran_india_gaul_realm_dejure.txt.
